Detailed core algorithm (p sum1):

function optimize(p, time_limit, beta_init, step_init):
   n=len(p), L=2n-1
   fft_n1, fft_n2 precomputed.
   best_p=p.copy(), best_score=local_score
   no_improve=0
   beta = beta_init (scale relative to maxC) e.g. 20.
   step = step_init e.g. 1.0.
   last=time
   for iter:
      C=conv_self(p, fft_n1)
      M=max(C); ss=sum(C)
      score=2*n*M/ss
      if score < best_score - 1e-9: best...
      if time over break
      # weights:
      r=C/M
      # Clip r to avoid large exp where C tiny: min r 0.
      vals = beta*(r-1)
      vals = np.maximum(vals, -50)
      w = np.exp(vals); w /= w.sum()
      # effective active eff=1/sum(w^2)
      eff=1/np.sum(w*w)
      # adapt beta to keep eff ~ target_eff maybe 100-1000
      # Since C values at peaks; if eff too small (<50), lower beta; if >2000, increase.
      if eff < 30: beta *= 0.8
      elif eff > 3000: beta *= 1.5
      else maybe keep; optionally slowly increase beta if improving: beta = min(beta*1.01, 5000)
      # d = corr(p,w)
      d = corr(p,w, fft_n2)
      # Ensure finite
      center = d - d.mean()
      scale = max(d - d.min(), - (d.max()-? )? use max(np.abs(center)).max)
      if scale < 1e-15: break
      center_scaled = center / scale
      # direction multiplicative:
      for alpha in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125, step*0.0625]:
          f = np.exp(-alpha * center_scaled)
          q = p * f
          qsum=q.sum()
          if qsum<=0: continue
          q/=qsum
          # optional: ensure no coefficient >1000/n? outline sum1, p_i max1, okay.
          # local score
          Cq=conv_self(q, fft_n1)
          Mq=M? max; ssum= Cq.sum()
          if ssum<=0: continue
          scoreq=2*n*Mq/ssum
          if scoreq < score - 1e-12:
              p=q.accept; step = min(step*1.1, max_step?)
              break
      else:
          # no improvement: reduce step
          step *= 0.5
          no_improve +=1
          if step < 1e-4 or no_improve > 20:
              # perturb or restart
              # Random smooth perturbation:
              # Add Gaussian low-freq to log p:
              logp = np.log(p + 1e-15)
              # generate noise with few Fourier modes
              m=min(20,n)
              noise = np.zeros(n)
              for k in range(1,m+1): noise += rng.normal(scale=1.0/(k+1)) * np.cos(2πk i/n + phase)
              logp += 0.02*stuck * noise / (std(noise)+eps)
              p = exp(logp); p/=sum
              beta *=? step=1; no_improve=0
      # Maybe every 100 iterations enforce p positive floor? Multiplicative stays positive.
   return best_p

import numpy as np, time, math

def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length() if m>1 else 1

def _conv_self(a, fft_n):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_n)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A, fft_n)
    C=C[:2*len(a)-1]
    C[C<0 & small]=0? Use np.maximum(C,0.0) maybe removes tiny negative; sum slightly.
    return C

def _score_from_C(C,n):
    s=C.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return inf
    return 2.0*n*C.max()/s

def _corr_p_w(p,w, fft_n):
    L=p.size
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], fft_n)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*W, fft_n)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]

def _normalize(p, cap=None):
   s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return ones; p=p/s; return p

def _smooth_random_p(n, rng, amp=0.3):
   x = np.arange(n)
   p = np.ones(n)
   K = min(12, n//2)
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      amp_k = amp / (k+1)
      p += amp_k * rng.normal() * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   p = np.maximum(p, 1e-3)
   return p/p.sum()
```

```
def _local_score(p,n):
   fft_n = _next_pow2(3*n-2) # maybe compute each; for inits same n.
   P = rfft(p, fft_n)
   C = irfft(P*P, fft_n)[:2*n-1]
   M = max(C)
   S = p.sum()
   if S <=0: return inf
   return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```

Now, optimize function details:
```
def _optimize(p, n, end_time, rng, best_local_holder=None):
   fft_n = _fft_size(n)
   L=2*n-1
   p = np.asarray(p,float).copy()
   s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)/n else p/=s
   # ensure positive floor? p = np.maximum(p, 1e-15); p/=p.sum()
   P = rfft...
   C = ...
   M=C.max(); score=2*n*M
   best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
   beta = 30.0
   step = 1.0
   no_improve = 0
   it = 0
   while time.monotonic() < end_time:
      it +=1
      P = rfft(p, fft_n)
      C=...
      M=C.max(); score=2*n*M
      if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
      # if score close to best and scale small maybe break? continue
      # weights
      if M <= 0: break
      r=C/M
      vals=beta*(r-1.0)
      vals=np.maximum(vals, -60.0)  # exp underflow not needed but limit effective? -60 makes tail 8e-27; okay. If beta large, okay. Use -745? If use -60, for beta big values below M-0.2/beta weight 1e-26; fine.
      w=np.exp(vals); ws=w.sum(); if ws<=0 or bad: w zeros? handle
      w/=ws
      # d
      W=rfft(w[::-1], fft_n)
      conv=irfft(P*W, fft_n)
      d=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
      mean_d = d.mean()
      center = d - mean_d
      center_max = np.max(np.abs(center))
      if center_max <= 1e-15 * max(1.0, abs(mean_d)):
          # too flat; perturb or lower beta
          beta *= 0.7; no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 30: perturb
          continue
      # adapt beta
      eff = 1.0 / (np.dot(w,w)+1e-300)
      if eff < 20: beta*=0.8
      elif eff > 1000: beta*=1.2
      else: beta*=1.01
      beta = min(max(beta,2.0),2000.0)
      # line search
      accepted=False
      c_max = center/center_max
      for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
          alpha=step*factor
          if alpha <=1e-12: continue
          z = -alpha * c_max
          # Optional mix to avoid large: z = np.clip(z,-50,50)
          f = np.exp(z)
          q = p * f
          qs = q.sum()
          if not finite or qs<=0: continue
          q /= qs
          # local score
          Pq = rfft(q, fft_n)
          Cq = irfft(Pq*Pq, fft_n)[:L]
          Mq = Cq.max()
          # sumq is 1 (q normalized), but use sumq
          scoreq = 2.0*n*Mq
          if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-13:
              p = q
              best_score? Maybe scoreq might not update best until next loop; but we can update now if scoreq < best_score.
              if scoreq < best_score:
                  best_p=p.copy(); best_score=scoreq
              no_improve=0
              step = min(4.0, alpha*1.25)
              break
      else:
          no_improve += 1
          step *= 0.5
          if step < 1e-4 or no_improve > 40:
              # perturb and reset
              _perturb(p, rng, no_improve)

def _active_weights(C,n,beta):
   M=C.max()
   if M<=0: return zeros, M
   r=C/M
   vals=beta*(r-1)
   vals=np.maximum(vals, -745) # no underflow
   w=np.exp(vals)
   ws=w.sum()
   if not finite or ws<=0: w=np.zeros_like(C); w[argmax]=1
   else w/=ws
   return w, M
```

Perturb function:
```
def _perturb(p, rng, intensity):
   n=p.size
   # low-pass noise
   L=n
   x=np.arange(n)
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   K=min(10, n//2)
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      noise += rng.normal(scale=1.0/np.sqrt(k)) * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x/n + rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi))
   if noise.std()>0: noise = 0.05*noise/noise.std()
   logp=np.log(p+1e-15) + noise
   np=np.exp(logp); np/=np.sum()
   # If any zero okay
   return np
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
        s = p.sum()
    return p / s
```

`_conv_from_P`:
```
def _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n):
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * P, fft_n)
    C = C[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negative
    # C[C < 0] = 0 may scan; okay
    if C.size:
        np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    return C
```
But `np.maximum(C,0,out=C)` okay.

`_self_fft_score`: maybe for local init:
```
def _local_score(p, n, fft_n=None):
    p = np.asarray(p)
    S = p.sum()
    if S <= 1e-300: return inf
    if fft_n is None: fft_n = _next_pow2(3*n-2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
    M = C.max()
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <=0: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
For p normalized sum1, denominator 1.

`_candidate_list`:
```
def _p_to_list(p, n):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64) * float(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): a = np.ones(n)
    m = a.max()
    if m > 1000.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / m)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    # If sum too small after clip? scale up? scale invariant but if max<1000, no. If sum<0.01, scale by 0.01/sum? But scale up could exceed cap. If sum <0.01, set [1,1].
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
       # scale to sum 1 maybe max >? n small
       if a.size > 0 and m > 0:
           scale = 1.0 / max(a.max(), 1e-300)
           a *= scale
           m2=a.max()
           if m2>1000: a*=1000/m2
       else: a = np.ones(2)
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
But if a max <1000 and sum <0.01 (if n huge and p sum1? sum=n, no). For n=1, a=1, sum1. Good.

`_exact_score_list(lst)` just evaluate.

Perturb:
```
def _smooth_perturb(p, rng, amp):
    n = p.size
    if n <= 2:
        q = p + rng.normal(0, amp, n)
        return _normalize_p(q)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = min(12, max(1, n//4))
    for k in range(1, K+1):
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2*np.pi)
        amp_k = amp / (0.5 + k)
        noise += amp_k * np.cos((2*np.pi*k*idx/n) + phase)
    # add small white floor to allow support changes?
    noise += rng.normal(0.0, 1e-3, n)
    q = p + noise
    return _normalize_p(q)
```
But linear perturbation may create zeros; normalize clips. Multiplicative log perturbation maybe better. Use log perturbation:
```
logp = np.log(p + 1e-15)
logp += noise
q = np.exp(logp); return normalize
```
If noise negative large, exp small; okay. Use amp 0.05.

Now, `_optimize`:
Need pass candidates callback? Simpler return best p/s. But to store candidates from inside improvements, outer can just take final best from each attempt. But if early break, best stored. If optimize time long, inner best local will be returned. Good. No need store every improvement. But final candidates if multiple attempts: each attempt returns best p; add to candidates. Initial inits also. Good.

`_optimize` signature:
```
def _optimize(p, n, end_time, rng, max_no_improve=500, verbose=False):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    fft_n = _next_pow2(3*n-2)
    L = 2*n -1
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
    M = C.max()
    score = 2.0*n*M  # sum p=1
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score
    best_time = time.monotonic()
    beta = 20.0
    step = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    iter_count=0
    last_perturb = time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        iter_count +=1
        if not np.isfinite(score) or M <=0:
             break
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; best_time=time.monotonic()
        # weights adapt maybe ensure eff
        # If M tiny? no
        # compute weights in small loop to adjust beta if eff bad
        for _ in range(3):
            r = C / M
            np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0, out=r)
            vals = beta * (r - 1.0)
            vals = np.maximum(vals, -745.0)
            w = np.exp(vals)
            ws = w.sum()
            if ws <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0; ws=1.0
            w /= ws
            eff = 1.0 / max(np.dot(w,w), 1e-300)
            if eff < 8 and beta > 2.0:
                beta = max(2.0, beta*0.7)
                continue
            if eff > 5000:
                beta = min(5000.0, beta*1.4)
                continue
            break
        beta = min(max(beta, 2.0), 5000.0)
        # gradient
        W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], fft_n)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * W, fft_n)
        d = conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
        mean_d = float(d.mean())
        center = d - mean_d
        center_max = float(np.max(np.abs(center)))
        mean_abs = max(1.0, abs(mean_d))
        if center_max <= 1e-14 * mean_abs:
            no_improve +=1
            beta = max(2.0, beta*0.7)
            if no_improve > 20 and time.monotonic() - last_perturb > 20.0:
                p = _smooth_perturb(p, rng, 0.04 + 0.005*min(5,no_improve//10))
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
                C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
                M = C.max(); score = 2.0*n*M
                if score < best_score:
                    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
                no_improve=0; last_perturb=time.monotonic(); beta=float(rng.uniform(5,100))
            if no_improve > 100:
                break
            continue
        # adapt beta (slow)
        if no_improve > 10:
             beta = max(2.0, beta*0.85)
        else:
             beta = min(5000.0, beta*1.005)
        accepted = False
        # gradient multiplicative candidates
        cmax = center / center_max
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            alpha = step * factor
            if alpha <= 1e-12: break
            z = -alpha * cmax
            # z in [-alpha, alpha]; if step>50? no
            q = p * np.exp(z)
            qs = q.sum()
            if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0: continue
            q /= qs
            Pq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_n)
            Cq = _conv_from_P(Pq, fft_n, n)
            Mq = float(Cq.max())
            scoreq = 2.0*n*Mq
            if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-12:
                p = q; P = Pq; C = Cq; score = scoreq; M = Mq
                if score < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score; best_time = time.monotonic()
                step = min(4.0, alpha * 1.25)
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                # optionally if accepted small, break; yes
                break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            step *= 0.6
            # inverse direction periodically
            if (no_improve % 3 == 0) or (no_improve > 5):
                # compute c
                pref = np.empty(L+1, dtype=np.float64)
                pref[0]=0.0
                np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
                idx = np.arange(n)
                c = pref[idx + n] - pref[idx]
                cmaxv = float(c.max())
                if np.isfinite(cmaxv) and cmaxv > 0:
                    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-10 * cmaxv)
                    base = 1.0 / c
                    bs = base.sum()
                    if np.isfinite(bs) and bs > 0:
                        base /= bs
                        for t in (0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                            q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * base
                            qs = q.sum()
                            if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0: continue
                            q /= qs
                            Pq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_n)
                            Cq = _conv_from_P(Pq, fft_n, n)
                            Mq = float(Cq.max())
                            scoreq = 2.0*n*Mq
                            if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-12:
                                p = q; P = Pq; C = Cq; score = scoreq; M = Mq
                                if scoreq < best_score:
                                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = scoreq; best_time = time.monotonic()
                                step = 1.0
                                no_improve = 0
                                accepted = True
                                break
            if no_improve > 25 and time.monotonic() - last_perturb > 15.0:
                amp = 0.03 + 0.005 * min(10, no_improve // 5)
                p = _smooth_perturb(p, rng, amp)
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
                C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
                M = C.max(); score = 2.0*n*M
                if score < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score; best_time = time.monotonic()
                no_improve = 0
                last_perturb = time.monotonic()
                beta = float(rng.uniform(5, 200))
                step = 1.0
            if no_improve > 400 and time.monotonic() - best_time > 180.0:
                break

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    margin = min(60.0, max(10.0, 0.1*budget_s))
    hard_deadline = start + max(5.0, budget_s - margin)
    finish_deadline = start + max(5.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    candidates = [] # list (local_score, p, n)
    best_exact = float('inf')
    best_exact_list = [1.0,1.0]
    def add_candidate(p,n,score):
       if p is None or score is None: return
       if not np.isfinite(score): return
       # ensure n int
       p2 = _normalize_p(p)
       candidates.append([float(score), p2.copy(), int(n)])
       if len(candidates) > 10:
           # remove worst (max score)
           candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
           del candidates[-1:]
    def try_exact_list(lst):
       nonlocal best_exact,best_exact_list
       if lst is None: return
       try:
          sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
       except Exception:
          return
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
           best_exact=sc; best_exact_list=lst
```

Let's draft final code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

TARGET_BOUND = 1.50274349

def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(2, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
    return p / s

...
```

In `_normalize_p`, if all zero, returns ones size p, sum1. Good.

`_conv_from_P`:
```
def _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n):
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * P, fft_n)
    L = 2*n - 1
    C = C[:L]
    C = np.where(C > 0.0, C, 0.0)
    return C
```
Using np.where allocates; could use maximum. For clarity:
```
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    return C
```
But `C` may not be writeable? irfft returns writeable. Slice view writeable? yes if contiguous. To be safe, `C = np.maximum(C[:L], 0.0)` returns new. Use that. It allocates but fine.
```
    C = C[:L].copy()
    np.maximum(C,0.0,out=C)
```
Need copy to ensure contiguous? Slicing first L is contiguous, but .copy ensures own. Good.

`_local_score`:
```
def _local_score(p, n, fft_n=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if p.size < 1 or S <= 1e-300:
        return float('inf')
    if fft_n is None:
        fft_n = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
    M = float(C.max()) if C.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S*S))
```

`_smooth_perturb`:
```
def _smooth_perturb(p, rng, amp):
    n = p.size
    if n <= 2:
        q = p + rng.normal(0.0, amp, n)
        return _normalize_p(q)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = min(12, max(1, n // 4))
    for k in range(1, K+1):
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
        scale = amp / (0.5 + 0.35*k)
        noise += scale * float(rng.normal()) * np.cos((2.0*np.pi*k*idx/n) + phase)
    noise += rng.normal(0.0, 0.001, n)
    q = np.log(p + 1e-15) + noise
    # avoid underflow
    q = np.clip(q, -50.0, 50.0)
    q = np.exp(q)
    return _normalize_p(q)
```
Clipping log to 50 maybe if p log ~ -10, noise small, clip not. Good. If p has zeros, log 1e-15, plus negative noise, exp small but not zero. Good.

Now `_optimize` code. Need be careful with `end_time` may be earlier than now.
```
def _optimize(p, n, end_time, rng):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= end_time:
        fft_n = _next_pow2(3*n-2) if n>0 else 1
        sc = _local_score(p,n,fft_n)
        return p, sc
    fft_n = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    L = 2*n - 1
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
    M = float(C.max()) if C.size else float('inf')
    score = float(2.0 * n * M)  # p sum1
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = float('inf')
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_time = time.monotonic()
    beta = 20.0
    step = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    last_perturb = time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        if not np.isfinite(score) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score; best_time = time.monotonic()
        # weights
        for _ in range(3):
            r = C / M
            np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0, out=r)
            vals = beta * (r - 1.0)
            vals = np.maximum(vals, -745.0)
            w = np.exp(vals)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
                ws = 1.0
            w /= ws
            eff = 1.0 / max(float(np.dot(w, w)), 1e-300)
            if eff < 8.0 and beta > 2.0:
                beta = max(2.0, beta * 0.7)
                continue
            if eff > 5000.0 and beta < 5000.0:
                beta = min(5000.0, beta * 1.4)
                continue
            break
        beta = float(min(5000.0, max(2.0, beta)))
        W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], fft_n)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * W, fft_n)
        d = conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1].copy()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
        mean_d = float(d.mean())
        center = d - mean_d
        center_max = float(np.max(np.abs(center)))
        scale_ref = max(1.0, abs(mean_d))
        if center_max <= 1e-14 * scale_ref:
            no_improve += 1
            beta = max(2.0, beta * 0.7)
            if no_improve > 20 and time.monotonic() - last_perturb > 20.0:
                p = _smooth_perturb(p, rng, 0.03 + 0.004*min(5, no_improve//10))
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
                C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
                M = float(C.max())
                score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score; best_time = time.monotonic()
                no_improve = 0
                last_perturb = time.monotonic()
                beta = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 200.0))
                step = 1.0
            if no_improve > 80 and time.monotonic() - best_time > 60.0:
                break
            continue
        # adapt beta slightly
        if no_improve > 10:
            beta = max(2.0, beta * 0.85)
        else:
            beta = min(5000.0, beta * 1.005)
        accepted = False
        cmax = center / center_max
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            alpha = step * factor
            if alpha <= 1e-12:
                break
            z = -alpha * cmax
            q = p * np.exp(z)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0.0:
                continue
            q = q / qs
            Pq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_n)
            Cq = _conv_from_P(Pq, fft_n, n)
            Mq = float(Cq.max())
            scoreq = float(2.0 * n * Mq)
            if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-12:
                p = q; P = Pq; C = Cq; M = Mq; score = scoreq
                if scoreq < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = scoreq; best_time = time.monotonic()
                step = min(4.0, alpha * 1.25)
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            step *= 0.6
            if (no_improve % 3 == 0) or no_improve > 5:
                pref = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
                pref[0] = 0.0
                np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
                idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
                c = pref[idx + n] - pref[idx]
                cmaxv = float(c.max())
                if np.isfinite(cmaxv) and cmaxv > 0.0:
                    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-10 * cmaxv)
                    base = 1.0 / c
                    bs = float(base.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(bs) and bs > 0.0:
                        base /= bs
                        for t in (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125):
                            if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
                                break
                            q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * base
                            qs = float(q.sum())
                            if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0.0:
                                continue
                            q = q / qs
                            Pq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_n)
                            Cq = _conv_from_P(Pq, fft_n, n)
                            Mq = float(Cq.max())
                            scoreq = float(2.0 * n * Mq)
                            if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-12:
                                p = q; P = Pq; C = Cq; M = Mq; score = scoreq
                                if scoreq < best_score:
                                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = scoreq; best_time = time.monotonic()
                                step = 1.0
                                no_improve = 0
                                accepted = True
                                break
            if not accepted and no_improve > 25 and time.monotonic() - last_perturb > 15.0:
                amp = 0.03 + 0.004 * min(10, no_improve // 5)
                p = _smooth_perturb(p, rng, amp)
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
                C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
                M = float(C.max())
                score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score; best_time = time.monotonic()
                no_improve = 0
                last_perturb = time.monotonic()
                beta = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 200.0))
                step = 1.0
            if no_improve > 400 and time.monotonic() - best_time > 180.0:
                break
    return best_p, float(best_score)
```

Check `_optimize`: If initial score inf? p normalized, M>0. Good.

One issue: In local score, for p sum1, `score=2*n*M`; but after perturbation, p normalized sum1. After line search q normalized. Good.

Potential issue: In `_optimize`, after no accepted and perturbation, we reset no_improve. But if perturbation scoreq is not finite? `_smooth_perturb` normalizes, should finite. If not, score inf, best remains; next loop score inf break? M? If score inf, while top not finite break. Good.

Potential issue: In inverse direction, `pref` length L+1, `idx + n` max 2n-1 = L, valid. `idx` int64, n int. Good.

Potential issue: `base = 1.0 / c`; if c has zeros, max gives eps. Good.

Now, `_p_to_list`:
```
def _p_to_list(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return [1.0]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
       # if tiny, scale to sum 1 unless max would exceed cap? p max maybe tiny too.
       if p.size > 0 and p.max() > 0:
           p = p / p.max()
       else:
           p = np.ones(2)
       s = float(p.sum())
       if s < 0.01:
           p = np.ones(2)
    return [float(x) for x in p]
```
But scaling by max changes score if p sum<0.01 and local score? This fallback rare. If p sum1, no.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

TARGET_BOUND = 1.50274349


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(2, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def _conv_from_P(P, fft_n: int, n: int):
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * P, fft_n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    C = C[:L].copy()
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    return C


def _local_score(p, n: int, fft_n: int = None) -> float:
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if n < 1 or S <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    if fft_n is None:
        fft_n = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
    M = float(C.max()) if C.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def _smooth_perturb(p, rng, amp: float):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n <= 2:
        return _normalize_p(p + rng.normal(0.0, amp, n))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = min(12, max(1, n // 4))
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        scale = amp / (0.5 + 0.35 * k)
        noise += scale * float(rng.normal()) * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * k * idx / n) + phase)
    noise += rng.normal(0.0, 1e-3, n)

    q = np.log(p + 1e-15) + noise
    q = np.clip(q, -50.0, 50.0)
    q = np.exp(q)
    return _normalize_p(q)


def _optimize(p, n: int, end_time: float, rng):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= end_time:
        fft_n = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2) if n > 0 else 1
        return p, _local_score(p, n, fft_n)

    fft_n = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
    C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
    M = float(C.max()) if C.size else float("inf")
    score = float(2.0 * n * M)

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = float("inf")

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_time = time.monotonic()

    beta = 20.0
    step = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    last_perturb = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        if not np.isfinite(score) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_score = score
            best_time = time.monotonic()

        # Adaptive log-sum-exp active weights for the convolution peak.
        for _ in range(3):
            r = C / M
            np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0, out=r)
            vals = beta * (r - 1.0)
            vals = np.maximum(vals, -745.0)
            w = np.exp(vals)

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0

            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
                ws = 1.0
            w /= ws

            eff = 1.0 / max(float(np.dot(w, w)), 1e-300)
            if eff < 8.0 and beta > 2.0:
                beta = max(2.0, beta * 0.7)
                continue
            if eff > 5000.0 and beta < 5000.0:
                beta = min(5000.0, beta * 1.4)
                continue
            break

        beta = float(min(5000.0, max(2.0, beta)))

        # Gradient component d_j = sum_q p_q w_{j+q}.
        W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], fft_n)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * W, fft_n)
        d = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].copy()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)

        mean_d = float(d.mean())
        center = d - mean_d
        center_max = float(np.max(np.abs(center)))
        scale_ref = max(1.0, abs(mean_d))

        if center_max <= 1e-14 * scale_ref:
            no_improve += 1
            beta = max(2.0, beta * 0.7)
            if no_improve > 20 and time.monotonic() - last_perturb > 20.0:
                p = _smooth_perturb(p, rng, 0.03 + 0.004 * min(5, no_improve // 10))
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
                C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
                M = float(C.max())
                score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_score = score
                    best_time = time.monotonic()
                no_improve = 0
                last_perturb = time.monotonic()
                beta = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 200.0))
                step = 1.0
            if no_improve > 80 and time.monotonic() - best_time > 60.0:
                break
            continue

        if no_improve > 10:
            beta = max(2.0, beta * 0.85)
        else:
            beta = min(5000.0, beta * 1.005)

        accepted = False
        cmax = center / center_max

        # Multiplicative projected-gradient-like retraction with backtracking.
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            alpha = step * factor
            if alpha <= 1e-12:
                break
            z = -alpha * cmax
            q = p * np.exp(z)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0.0:
                continue
            q = q / qs

            Pq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_n)
            Cq = _conv_from_P(Pq, fft_n, n)
            Mq = float(Cq.max())
            scoreq = float(2.0 * n * Mq)

            if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-12:
                p = q
                P = Pq
                C = Cq
                M = Mq
                score = scoreq
                if scoreq < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_score = scoreq
                    best_time = time.monotonic()
                step = min(4.0, alpha * 1.25)
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            step *= 0.6

            # Alternate MM-style direction from the sliding-window weights.
            if (no_improve % 3 == 0) or no_improve > 5:
                pref = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
                pref[0] = 0.0
                np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
                idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
                c = pref[idx + n] - pref[idx]
                cmaxv = float(c.max())
                if np.isfinite(cmaxv) and cmaxv > 0.0:
                    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-10 * cmaxv)
                    base = 1.0 / c
                    bs = float(base.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(bs) and bs > 0.0:
                        base /= bs
                        for t in (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125):
                            if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
                                break
                            q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * base
                            qs = float(q.sum())
                            if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0.0:
                                continue
                            q = q / qs

                            Pq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_n)
                            Cq = _conv_from_P(Pq, fft_n, n)
                            Mq = float(Cq.max())
                            scoreq = float(2.0 * n * Mq)

                            if np.isfinite(scoreq) and scoreq < score - 1e-12:
                                p = q
                                P = Pq
                                C = Cq
                                M = Mq
                                score = scoreq
                                if scoreq < best_score:
                                    best_p = p.copy()
                                    best_score = scoreq
                                    best_time = time.monotonic()
                                step = 1.0
                                no_improve = 0
                                accepted = True
                                break

            if not accepted and no_improve > 25 and time.monotonic() - last_perturb > 15.0:
                amp = 0.03 + 0.004 * min(10, no_improve // 5)
                p = _smooth_perturb(p, rng, amp)
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_n)
                C = _conv_from_P(P, fft_n, n)
                M = float(C.max())
                score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_score = score
                    best_time = time.monotonic()
                no_improve = 0
                last_perturb = time.monotonic()
                beta = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 200.0))
                step = 1.0

            if no_improve > 400 and time.monotonic() - best_time > 180.0:
                break

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _p_to_list(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return [1.0]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        if p.size > 0 and float(p.max()) > 0.0:
            p = p / float(p.max())
        else:
            p = np.ones(2, dtype=np.float64)
        if float(p.sum()) < 0.01:
            p = np.ones(2, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    margin = min(60.0, max(10.0, 0.1 * budget_s))
    hard_deadline = start + max(5.0, budget_s - margin)
    finish_deadline = start + max(5.0, budget_s - 2.0)

    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_exact_list = [1.0, 1.0]
    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(p, sc):
        if p is None or sc is None:
            return
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        p2 = _normalize_p(p)
        candidates.append([float(sc), p2.copy()])
        if len(candidates) > 12:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            del candidates[-1]

    def try_exact(p):
        nonlocal best_exact, best_exact_list
        if p is None:
            return
        lst = _p_to_list(p)
        if not lst:
            return
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            return
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_exact_list = lst

    # Parent initialization, if provided.
    p_parent = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size >= 2:
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            S = float(arr.sum())
            if S > 0.01:
                p_parent = arr / S
                p_parent = p_parent / float(p_parent.sum())
    except Exception:
        p_parent = None

    if p_parent is not None:
        n = int(p_parent.size)
    else:
        n = 2048 if budget_s > 300.0 else 1024

    # Build a few cheap, diversity-preserving initializations.
    inits = []
    if p_parent is not None:
        inits.append(p_parent)

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for d in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
        p = 1.0 + d * (xs - 0.5)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-3)
        inits.append(p / float(p.sum()))

    for d in (0.5, 1.0):
        p = 1.0 + d * xs
        inits.append(p / float(p.sum()))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(2):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        K = min(12, max(1, n // 4))
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = 0.12 / (0.5 + 0.35 * k)
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            p += amp * float(rng.normal()) * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * k * idx / n) + phase)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-3)
        inits.append(p / float(p.sum()))

    fft_n = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    scored = []
    for init in inits:
        pinit = _normalize_p(init)
        sc = _local_score(pinit, n, fft_n)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append([sc, pinit])

    if not scored:
        pinit = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        scored.append([2.0, pinit])

    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    for sc, p in scored:
        add_candidate(p, sc)

    if p_parent is not None:
        try_exact(p_parent)

    attempts = [p for _, p in scored[:3]]

    try:
        if attempts and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20.0:
            start1 = time.monotonic()
            if len(attempts) == 1:
                end1 = hard_deadline - 5.0
            else:
                end1 = min(hard_deadline - 120.0, start1 + (hard_deadline - 5.0 - start1) * 0.75)
                end1 = max(start1 + 30.0, end1)

            p1, sc1 = _optimize(attempts[0], n, end1, rng)
            add_candidate(p1, sc1)
            try_exact(p1)

            early1 = time.monotonic() < end1 - 20.0
            if (
                best_exact > TARGET_BOUND
                and early1
                and len(attempts) > 1
                and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 180.0
            ):
                remaining = hard_deadline - 150.0 - time.monotonic()
                if remaining > 90.0:
                    end2 = min(hard_deadline - 150.0, time.monotonic() + remaining * 0.6)
                    p2, sc2 = _optimize(attempts[1], n, end2, rng)
                    add_candidate(p2, sc2)
                    try_exact(p2)

            # Final refinement: continue from the currently best exact candidate
            # when available, otherwise from the best local candidate.
            if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 45.0:
                pstart = None
                if np.isfinite(best_exact) and best_exact_list:
                    arr = np.asarray(best_exact_list, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                    if arr.size == n and float(arr.sum()) > 0.01:
                        pstart = _normalize_p(arr)
                if pstart is None and candidates:
                    candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
                    pstart = candidates[0][1].copy()
                if pstart is not None:
                    pF, scF = _optimize(pstart, n, hard_deadline - 5.0, rng)
                    add_candidate(pF, scF)
                    try_exact(pF)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Exact final validation of a small set of local best candidates.
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < finish_deadline - 10.0 and candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            evaluated = 0
            for sc, p in candidates:
                if time.monotonic() > finish_deadline - 10.0:
                    break
                if evaluated >= 6:
                    break
                if np.isfinite(best_exact) and sc > best_exact + 0.01 and evaluated > 0:
                    break
                try_exact(p)
                evaluated += 1
    except Exception:
        pass

    clean = []
    if np.isfinite(best_exact) and best_exact_list:
        for x in best_exact_list:
            try:
                xf = float(x)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if np.isfinite(xf):
                clean.append(max(0.0, min(1000.0, xf)))

    if not clean or sum(clean) < 0.01:
        clean = [1.0, 1.0]

    return clean
```
